A grown-up-sized long-sleeved, ankle-length, drop-hood zip-front onesie, with a big attached hood, with plain ribbed cuffs and no gloves, and with built-in slipper feet, in plush minky, made as a three-dimensional plush version of the outfit of a court wizard, with real layers of fabric and fabric-like materials. The chest and sleeves form a velvet robe with gold astrological embroidery, cut from cotton twill, velvet and chenille. A sewn-on hood is built as a tall pointed hat in faux leather. The feet are plush padded versions of slippers. The sleeves end in plain cuffs, with bare hands showing. A soft stuffed version of scroll is sewn onto the hip. Each part of the outfit is its own separate piece of fabric sewn onto a padded base, giving a stuffed, three-dimensional look. Skin shows at the face, hands and the open neckline, in __clothing_onesie_skin__. It is a cuddly, three-dimensional costume version of the outfit, with visible stitching, soft folds and a slightly puffy look, but still clearly a one-piece onesie. A full court wizard costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a velvet robe with gold astrological embroidery, a tall pointed hat (as the hood), slippers (as the feet), a scroll sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs, scalloped felt edging, embroidered patches and crests with stitched borders and mirrored sequin stars and shapes. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.
A grown-up-sized long-sleeved, ankle-length, drop-hood zip-front onesie, with a big attached hood, with simple wrist cuffs and no attached gloves, and footless with elasticated ankle cuffs, in soft fleece, made as a three-dimensional plush version of the outfit of a starship engineer, with real layers of fabric and fabric-like materials. On the body it is a grease-stained jumpsuit with tool loops, made of chenille layered with faux fur, with details in corduroy. The hood is a soft sculpted version of goggles, in tulle. The suit is footless, so the footwear is left off. The sleeves end in plain cuffs, with bare hands showing. A padded fabric replica of plasma torch hangs from the belt. The outfit is built up in overlapping layers, a soft padded body, appliqué panels sewn over it, and raised stitched trims on top. Skin shows at the face, hands and the open neckline, in __clothing_onesie_skin__. Every detail is real soft fabric with depth and texture, not a flat picture, seams, pockets, buttons and trims are all sewn on. It is a complete costume of starship engineer in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a grease-stained jumpsuit with tool loops, goggles (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a plasma torch sewn or attached to the suit. The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, satin ribbon bows and piping, terry-loop textured stripes, rhinestone buttons down the front and fabric flames, stars or stripes sewn along the sleeves and legs. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.

Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.

The attached gloves are padded fabric gloves in the outfit's trim colours, sewn onto the ends of the sleeves. A padded fabric replica of spear hangs from the belt. The outfit is built up in overlapping layers, a soft padded body, appliqué panels sewn over it, and raised stitched trims on top.
